- **h-archive**: Archive STANDARD/RESEARCH task at Phase 6/R3 — gate → WAL → move → DONE. TRIGGER: '归档' / Phase 6 boundary. NOT FOR: PATCH (rejected); PR (use /h-pr). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-archive.md) · ★14)

- **h-ci**: Ingest CI/CD failure → classify → route (debug task / incident) + failure_memory record. TRIGGER: red GitHub Actions run / 'CI 挂了'. NOT FOR: local test fail (use /h-fix-bug). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-ci.md) · ★14)
- **h-collab**: Generate a cross-team collaboration deliverable from task_brief — draft document, create collab state file, mark task pending external review ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-collab.md) · ★14)

- **h-context-check**: Pull-model context probe — recent events + recurring failures + active task + dirty diff + sensitive-surface scan + active insights. The canonical entry to gather context before deciding profile / scope / next action. TRIGGER: phase start / 不确定状态 / 进入未知领域. NOT FOR: every prompt (this is on-demand, not push). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-context-check.md) · ★14)
- **h-decompose**: Decompose PRD/EPIC → INVEST subtasks + per-task briefs + launch_spec rows. TRIGGER: multi-feature input / '拆分需求'. NOT FOR: single task (use /h-brief). ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-decompose.md) · ★14)
- **h-design**: Design step for HIGH-risk brief — dispatch system-architect, write ADRs, fill §8/§9. TRIGGER: HIGH-risk after /h-brief / '做设计'. NOT FOR: implementation. ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-design.md) · ★14)
- **h-distill**: Wiki cleanup — librarian scan → user approves → execute. ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-distill.md) · ★14)

- **h-evolve**: Turn a high-confidence insight into a concrete rule-change proposal. Pick insight → generate file/line edit suggestion → human approves → optional --apply. ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/h-evolve.md) · ★14)

- **habits**: Habit tracking. First run interviews you one question at a time to build a versioned habits profile — each habit with its own criteria (daily / N-per-week / N-per-month, build or limit) and a priority. Day-to-day tracking lives in dated markdown files (life/habit-tracking/&lt;date&gt;.md) you tick like a checklist; a SQLite store is synced from them for analysis. Afterward it shows your progress vs each criteria (✅ met / ⏳ not yet / ⚠️ over), top 20 by priority. Habits are auto-marked from your journaling + planning sessions. Once your diet + fitness profiles exist, the scored defaults "Eat clean"… ([baymac/pbrain](https://github.com/baymac/pbrain/blob/HEAD/commands/habits.md) · ★1)
